     February
     二月

          Onewo consolidated Dantian Property into its financial statements,
          officially entering the hospital and campus property management
          sectors.
          萬物雲併表丹田物業，正式進軍醫院物業及校園物業賽道。




     March
     三月

          Vanke Service and China Index Academy
          jointly released the industry’s first White
          Paper on the Development of Property
          Housekeepers. Centered around the
          comprehensive management service
          design of full housing lifecycle of
          customers, the White Paper reimagines
          community life through diversified service
          linkages and presents a new service model
          for building happy communities, serving
          as an industry benchmark.
          萬科物業、中指研究院聯合發佈行業
          首 份《 物 業 管 家 發 展 白 皮 書 》，《 白 皮
          書》圍繞客戶住房全生命週期統籌管家
          服務設計，以多元化服務為鏈接點，
          重構鄰里美好生活，打造幸福社區命題下的服務新範本，具有行業示範意義。

April
四月

      Onewo’s Innovation Case of Energy
      Management in Chinese Residential
      Communities received the 2024 Climate
      Lighthouse Community Annual Pioneer
      Award at Shanghai Climate Week. The
      case proposed an innovative integrated
      solution of “hardware upgrades
      + algorithmic models + intelligent
      platforms” to enable smart energy
      transformation in managed communities.
      It has been applied to over 1,000
      residential communities nationwide, saving 72.45 million kWh of electricity annually and engaging
      approximately 1.87 million households in energy conservation and carbon reduction practices.
      萬物雲「中國人居住宅社區能源管理創新案例」獲2024 年「上海氣候週氣候燈塔社區年度領航
      獎」。該案例創新性地提出「硬件改造+算法模型+智能平台」綜合解決方案，以實現在管社區的
      能源智能化轉型，已推廣至全國1,000餘個住宅社區，實現年節約電量7,245萬kWh，引導約187
      萬戶中國家庭參與節能減排實踐。




May
五月

      Vanke Service, together with Shenzhen Property Management Association
      and CRIC, released the industry’s first Community Property Service Manual
      for Children. Centered on children’s safety and development, the Manual
      presents, for the first time, a comprehensive framework for community
      children’s services characterized by “full coverage, full touchpoints, and
      micro-services”.
    萬科物業聯合深圳物協、克而瑞發佈行業首份《社區兒童物業服務手
    冊》。《手冊》以兒童安全和成長為核心，首次對社區兒童服務進行了
    「全覆蓋、全觸點、微服務」的完整呈現。

     August
     八月

          Vanke Service and Meituan jointly
          launched a delivery rider access solution
          to address the last-100-meters challenge
          in delivery. Backed by technology
          coordination of both companies, the
          solution, based on “minimal necessary”
          data collection and “maximum possible”
          mutual support, balances efficient rider
          access with orderly visitor registration in
          residential communities, transforming an industry pain point into a service highlight.
          萬科物業&美團共同發佈騎手通行解決方案，合力破解配送最後一百米難題。這一解決方案背
          後，是雙方公司技術的配合，基於「最小必要」的數據收集，提供「最大可能」的雙向支持，既滿
          足了騎手通行的效率，也保障了小區訪客登記的秩序，將行業痛點變為服務亮點。




     September
     九月

          Onewo’s community commercial brand
          Oneshine launched its flagship product
          module “Oneshine Food Market” at
          Fuzhou Aoti Sunshine Paradise under the
          “Onewo Town + Business Community”
          strategy, becoming the “living room” of
          the Jianxin South Onewo Town.
          萬物雲社區商業品牌萬芊薈在福州奧體
          陽光天地推出「蝶城+社商」關鍵產品模
          塊「萬芊食集」，成為建新南蝶城「會客廳」。
          The Shanghai Stock Exchange and the Shenzhen Stock Exchange issued an announcement that, due
          to constituent adjustments to the Hang Seng Composite LargeCap, MidCap, and SmallCap Indexes,
          Onewo was included in the Southbound Stock Connect list. The inclusion took effect on the next
          Southbound Stock Connect trading day, allowing mainland investors to purchase the Company’s shares
          via Southbound Stock Connect, significantly enhancing liquidity.
          上交所、深交所發佈公告，因恒生綜合大型股指數、中型股指數、小型股指數實施成份股調整，
          萬物雲調入港股通標的名單，並自下一港股通交易日起生效，內地投資人可通過港股通購買公司
          股票，流動性得到顯著提升。

November
十一月

   Onewo’s case titled “Shanghai Jinse
   Liuxiang Garden: Building a Low-Carbon,
   Green Smart Community with ‘Lingshi’
   Edge Servers and Intelligent Microgrid
   Algorithms” was selected as a corporate
   climate action case under the Ministry
   of Ecology and Environment’s “2024
   Beautiful China, I’m a Contributor”
   initiative. It was also recognized by the
   Shanghai Municipal Ecology and Environment Bureau as a Low-Carbon Demonstration Community and
   was presented at the 29th United Nations Climate Change Conference (COP29) as a representative case
   from Chinese enterprises.
   萬物雲「上海金色榴鄉園基於 ‘ 靈石 ’ 邊緣服務器和智能微電網算法打造低碳、綠色的智慧社區」案
   例入選國家生態環境部「2024美麗中國，我是行動者」企業氣候行動案例，獲選上海市生態環境
   局低碳示範社區，並作為中國企業代表在第29屆聯合國應對氣候變化大會(COP29)上發言。

     December
     十二月

          Onewo hosted the “Looking Ahead”
          Brand Conference, where it released the
          “Flexible Pricing System” and the “Vanke
          Service Select” brand for residential space
          services to expand service price range;
          launched the professional campus and
          hospital property management brand
          “Dantian Property” for education and
          healthcare space services; and introduced
          the “Lingshi” edge server for real estate
          management, positioned as the AI brain for real estate operations, fundamentally transforming property
          service operations and reshaping space service efficiency.
          萬物雲舉辦「睿見」品牌大會，面向住宅空間發佈「彈性定價體系」、「萬科物業智選」品牌，拓寬
          服務價格空間；面向教育、醫療空間發佈校園、醫院物業專業品牌「丹田物業」；面向不動產管
          理，發佈邊緣服務器「靈石」，作為不動產管理的AI大腦，徹底改變物業服務作業形態，重塑空
          間效率。

          Cushman & Wakefield Vanke Service
          hosted its 5th anniversary event themed
          “Give Me Five”, where it launched
          smart and low-carbon solutions for real
          estate operations. Empowered by the AI
          brain Onewo “Lingshi”, these solutions
          help asset holders carry out assets
          management, corporate services, energy
          management, and property oversight
          more efficiently and transparently.
          萬物梁行舉辦成立五週年主題活動「Give Me Five」，發佈不動產運營的智慧+低碳解決方案，依
          托AI智慧大腦 — 萬物雲「靈石」，幫助資產持有者更高效、透明地開展資產管理、企業服務、能
          源管理和物業監管等。

Mr. Zhu Baoquan                            朱保全先生

manager

        致股東書



     Letter to Shareholders: The Price of AI                              致股東：AI的代價

     Is Deepseek seamlessly connected? Is the large language model        接入DeepSeek了嗎？本地部署大模型了
     (LLM) hosted on-premises? China’s AI boom has all of a sudden        嗎？屬於中國的AI盛宴一下子超越了2 年前
     surpassed the initial wave sparked by ChatGPT two years ago.         ChatGPT那波初始聲浪。在研究與實踐中，
     As I explore and experiment, I have been thinking about what AI      我一直在思考，之於我們這個行業與萬物
     means for our industry and Onewo.                                    雲，AI意味著什麼？

     AI has democratized access to general professional                   AI帶來了通用專業的平權。律師、會計師、
     expertise. In general professions such as law, accounting, and       品牌這些通用專業，往往越大的公司越有能
     branding, larger companies typically have greater ability to hire    力聘請薪酬更高、經驗更豐富的專業人士。
     higher-paid, more experienced professionals. However, with           但在AI加持之下，即便是小公司，也可以用
     AI, even small firms can access expert-level opinions or outputs     較低成本獲得不亞於專家的意見或者作品，
     at a lower cost, which is definitely beneficial in terms of costs    這對於毛利率並不高的物業行業，在費用層
     for the property services industry, where profit margins are not     面絕對是件好事。但專業仍然需要，因為大
     high. Yet, expertise remains essential, as large models require      模型需要專業的prompt，才能給出專業的答
     professional prompts to deliver professional answers.                案。

     It is difficult to fine-tune an industry-specific large model.       很難調出行業大模型。通過本地化部署幾
     After more than six months of testing by locally deploying           種大模型，做了大半年的嘗試，並沒有調
     multiple large models, we have not been able to fine-tune a          出令人滿意的專業大模型。於是改向 RAG
     satisfactory professional large model. Instead, we have shifted      (Retrieval-augmented Generation)路線，把
     to the Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) approach –               專業知識寫入向量數據庫，再根據需求調用
     embedding professional knowledge into a vector database and          不同大模型，據此做出的「智能物業管家」、
     retrieving different large models based on needs. The resulting      「智能IT管家」及「智能HR管家」已經應用於
     “Intelligent Property Manager,” “Intelligent IT Manager,” and        實戰。
     “Intelligent HR Manager” have already been deployed in practice.


     AI as a tool for labor substitution – or being replaced by           利用 A I 做人工替代或被甲方反替代。A I 可
     Party As instead. AI can bring obvious labor substitution,           以帶來顯而易見的人工替代，但值得警惕的
     but one must be cautious. Over the past two decades, due to          是，過去二十年由於專業化分工協作，很多
     professional specialization, many companies have outsourced          企業把非主營業務外包，比如科技公司代碼
     non-principal businesses, such as coding work by technology          開發的工作，當作為外包方試圖用AI做人工
     companies. However, when attempting to use AI for labor              替代時，切莫忘記你的甲方自己也可以用AI
     substitution as an outsourcing provider, never forget that Party     完成這項任務，而無須再外包給你。這時
     As can also use AI to complete the task, eliminating the need to     候，你與甲方會從協作，短期內會變成競爭
     outsource to you. In such cases, collaboration with Party As could   者，然後看誰更快、成本更低。
     quickly shift into competition, where speed and cost-effectiveness
     become decisive factors.

Robotics can significantly improve efficiency without                 機器人對提效有積極意義且不改變合同主

operations, significantly improving service efficiency. For routine   多產權人共有空間部分的日常打理與應急服

by property owners, there is no going back to the 1980s’ self-        似的自助，物業公司作為第三方被委託的需
service model of “Three Guarantees for the Area in Front of           求仍然剛需存在。
One’s Door”. The demand for property companies as third-party
service providers remains fundamental.


AI Agents may become the greatest threat to property                  智能體(AI AGENT)或是物業公司的最大威
services companies. The core of property services lies in             脅。如果說物業服務的核心，是通過與業主
reaching consensus with property owners and leveraging                達成共識，並依托專業知識指揮「四保」高
professional knowledge to direct the “Four Ensures” personnel         效作業，創造安全、安心的環境，助力資產
for efficient operations, in order to create a safe and secure        保值增值。那麼，是否可以基於數據創造一
environment and help preserve and enhance asset value. Then,          個物業項目的智能體，比如有三位住在一樓
is it possible to create an AI Agent for a property project based     的業主連續報事家中地漏返水，智能體便可
on data? For example, if three first-floor property owners            以判斷並發出工單，把所有污水管排查一遍
consecutively report backflow issues in their floor drains, the AI    看看是否有系統性問題。針對日常任務的跟
Agent could determine the issue and generate a work order to          進、臨時任務的督辦、工單調度與質檢、預
inspect all sewage pipes to check if there are systemic problems.     防性分析，這些物業經理的職責，都是可以
The abilities to follow up on routine tasks, oversee temporary        預判的智能體能力。
assignments, manage work order scheduling and quality control,
and conduct preventive analysis – these responsibilities of a
property manager are all capabilities that we can anticipate an AI
Agent will possess.


We look forward to making Onewo’s “Lingshi Edge Server” even          期待萬物雲的「靈石邊緣服務器」愈發智能。
smarter. Soon, the Company will have three categories of              公司很快就會衍生為三類崗位，H類為真
positions: H-type (human employees), R-type (robots), and A-type      人、R 類為機器人、A 類為智能體。作為企
(AI Agents). As a corporation, we must strive to take action now.     業現在就要努力去做，一方面做好企業知識
On the one hand, we must build a corporate knowledge base to          庫，幫助R類、A類員工上崗，一方面要盡快
support R-type and A-type employees in taking up their roles. On      區分哪些工作是必須的H類崗位，並提前做
the other hand, we must quickly identify which positions must         好轉崗培訓，在 R 類與 A 類員工上崗後提供

     This era leaves no time for rest. Just as the industry emerged      時代真是不容人休息片刻，行業剛剛渡劫開
     from the crisis brought by the connected transactions with          發商關連交易影響，又要搶跑大模型。過去
     developers, we are now facing a race to adopt large models.         三年，從開發商高增長的確定性，到與開發
     Over the past three years, from the certainty of high growth        商關連交易的冰與火，要收入沒現金，不要
     of developers to the volatility of connected transactions of        收入利潤下滑，種種業務的不確定性讓上市
     developers – if revenue is pursued, there is no cash; if revenue    物企只能用高分紅給股東定心。但如過去年
     is forgone, profits decline – the uncertainty in various business   報《致股東》所言，如果上市物企做好獨立性
     aspects has left listed property companies with no choice but       的準備，2025 年核心（非開發商）業務的增
     to set their shareholders’ minds at rest with high dividends.       幅會大於關聯方業務降幅，並呈現剪刀差，
     However, as we pointed out in the past letter to shareholders, if   2025年會是撥開雲霧的里程碑年。同時，頭
     listed property companies are well-prepared for independence,       部品牌企業市佔率會進一步集中：在住宅小
     the growth rate of their core (non-developer) businesses in         區更換物業的大趨勢中，頭部品牌企業呈現
     2025 will be greater than the decline rate of their related-party   顯著機會，而在非住領域的招投標中，越來
     businesses, presenting a scissors gap. 2025 will be a milestone     越多的甲方會提出業務交換條件，大概率只
     year for lifting the clouds. At the same time, the market share     有頭部品牌企業有機會接招。而伴隨近年因
     of leading enterprises will be further concentrated: in the trend   流動性、資產抵債等資產交易而帶來的所有
     of residential property replacement, leading enterprises have       者大更換，新業主對「智慧與綠色」對運營改
     significant opportunities, while in non-residential bidding, more   善的關注，更是給到具備「科技能力」的物業
     and more Party As propose B2B exchange conditions, but it’s         企業帶來替換服務的機會。
     most likely that only leading enterprises have the opportunity to
     accept the bid. With the replacement of asset owners in recent
     years due to liquidity challenges, debt-to-asset swaps, and other
     asset transactions, new property owners’ focus on “intelligence
     and green” for operational improvements has further opened
     opportunities for property enterprises with “technological
     capabilities” to replace services.

     Growth alone is not the compass of tomorrow; nurturing              並非業績增長即是未來，轉型中的能力建設
     capabilities through transition charts the course. “ Asset          至關重要。「資產管理能力，AI 應用能力，
     management capabilities, AI application capabilities, and green     綠色低碳能力」將作為萬物雲的關鍵能力建
     and low-carbon capabilities” will be Onewo’s key capacity-          設方向。在過去難熬的一年裡，陷入無端揣
     building directions. In the past challenging year, groundless       測不算痛心，因為真相如竹筍，雖無言卻總
     speculation was not distressing. Truth grows as a seed in           要破土。當看到國資向母公司的支持助力，
     darkness: wordless, but bound to reach the light. As we witness     眼中現出希望、淚光。
     the support from state-owned capital for our parent company,
     we finally see hope reflected in our eyes – moist with tears.

      RELATIONSHIP WITH STAKEHOLDERS                                       與持份者的關係
      The Group recognizes that its employees, customers and suppliers     本集團認識到，僱員、客戶及供應商對集團
      are critical to the sustainable development of the Group,            持續發展產生的重大意義，因此我們將與所
      therefore we will deeply integrate the synergetic development        有持份者協同發展深度融入公司治理體系。
      with all stakeholders into the corporate governance system.          通過完善參與機制、健全長效溝通平台、強
      We will build a more resilient and sustainable development           化價值創造能力，構建更具韌性的可持續發
      ecosystem by enhancing the participation mechanism, improving        展生態。本集團深入實施人才強企，將人力
      the long-term and effective communication platform and               資本增值作為企業高質量發展的核心動能。
      strengthening the value creation capability. The Group has           我們高度重視員工的整體福祉，致力於構建
      thoroughly implemented the strategy of talent-based corporate        公平、安全、包容的工作場所，並確保他們
      development, and regarded the appreciation of human capital as       因個人成績獲得公正的認可和獎勵，以及豐
      the core driver for high-quality development of the enterprise.      富的職業成長空間。
      We attach great importance to the overall well-being of our
      employees and are committed to creating a fair, safe and
      inclusive workplace, ensuring that they are fairly recognized and
      rewarded for their individual achievements and ample career
      growth space.


      In order to constantly improve employees’ professional capability    為了持續提升僱員的專業能力和綜合素質，
      and overall quality, the Group will systematically establish a       本集團系統構建高質量培訓與發展體系，確

Onewo built a broad development platform for employees.              萬物雲為員工搭建了廣闊的發展平台，根據

1.    “Onewo Dream” Development                                      1. 「萬紫千紅」好發展

      The Group has launched the employee development                  本集團自2019 年起推出員工發展項
      project since 2019 and built a multi-path development            目，面向基層員工構建專業化、多元
      mechanism with professionalization, diversification and full     化、全業務場景的多路徑發展機制，
      business scenarios for front line employees, to encourage        鼓勵一線基層員工，探索職業新道

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                    股息政策
The declaration and payment of dividends shall be determined       宣派及派付股息將由董事會全權酌情釐定。
at the sole discretion of the Board. The Board shall also take     於考慮是否建議派付股息及釐定股息金額
into account the following factors when considering whether        時，董事會亦應考慮下列因素：我們的經營
to propose dividends and determining the dividend amount:          業績、現金流量、財務狀況、附屬公司向我
our results of operations, cash flows, financial condition,        們派付的現金股息、業務前景、有關我們宣
payments by our subsidiaries of cash dividends to us, business     派及派付股息的法定、監管及合約限制、總

conditions, business cycle of the Group’s business and other       其他內部或外部因素，以及董事會可能認為
internal or external factors that may have an impact on the        重要的其他因素。股息僅可自我們可供合法
business or financial performance and position of the Group, as    分派的利潤中宣派或派付。
well as other factors that our Board may consider important. No

3.   Chairman and Chief Executive Officer                       3.   主席與行政總裁

     Pursuant to code provision C.2.1 of the CG Code, the            根 據《 企 業 管 治 守 則 》守 則 條 文 第
     roles of chairman and chief executive should be separate        C.2.1條，主席與行政總裁的角色應有

     Mr. Zhu Baoquan is the chairman of our Board and the            全先生為本公司董事長兼總經理，且

     In view of the fact that Mr. Zhu Baoquan has been               鑒於自2011 年2 月起朱保全先生一直

         Board diversity policy                                          董事會多元化政策

         The Company regards the board diversity policy as a key         本公司將董事會的多元化政策視為推
         driver of sustainable development. In order to enhance          動可持續發展的重要驅動力。為提升
         the operational efficiency of the Board and to maintain         董事會的運作效率並保持卓越的企業
         an excellent standard of corporate governance, the              管治水平，本公司積極推行董事會多
         Company has actively implemented a board diversity              元化政策，明確提出了實現和保持董
         policy which clearly sets out the approach to achieve and       事會多元化的途徑。根據這一政策，
         maintain diversity in our Board. Pursuant to this policy,       我們在甄選董事會候選人時，會綜
         selection of Board candidates will take into account a          合考慮多種多元化因素，如性別、年
         wide range of diversity factors, such as gender, age,           齡、文化及教育背景、行業經驗、技
         cultural and educational background, industry experience,       術能力、專業資格與技能、知識儲
         technical capabilities, professional qualifications and         備、服務年限等，同時充分考慮本集
         skills, knowledge and length of service, etc. We will also      團獨特的業務模式和特定需求。在最
         fully consider the Group’s unique business model and            終確定董事候選人時，我們將以候選
         special needs. When finalizing Director candidates, the         人的優勢和能為董事會帶來的價值為
         candidates’ advantages and the value that they can bring        主要考慮標準。通過這一系列的舉
         to our Board will be our primary consideration. We are          措，我們致力於構建一個多元化、高
         committed to building a diverse, efficient and dynamic          效且充滿活力的董事會，為本集團的
         Board that will provide strong support for the long-term        長期發展提供有力保障。
         development of the Group through these initiatives.

         of the diversity policy to be effective. The diversity policy   施屬有效。多元化政策妥為實施，其

STATEMENTS

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether        我們的目標是對合併財務報表整體是否不存
the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from         在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取
material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to           得合理保證，並出具包括我們意見在內的核
issue an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. Our report is     數師報告。我們的報告僅向閣下（作為整體）
made solely to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do         作出，除此之外別無其他用途。我們不會就
not assume responsibility towards or accept liability to any other     本報告的內容向任何其他人士負上或承擔任
person for the contents of this report.                                何責任。

Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a        合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按照
guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs             《香港審計準則》進行的審計總能發現存在某
will always detect a material misstatement when it exists.             一重大錯誤陳述。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯
Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are considered         誤引起，如果合理預期其單獨或匯總起來可
material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably   能影響合併財務報表使用者依賴合併財務報
be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken         表所作出的經濟決定，則有關的錯誤陳述可
on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.               被視作重大。

     limited liability under the Company Law of the PRC.              司根據《中華人民共和國公司法》改制
                                                                      為股份有限公司。

     During the year, the Group was involved in the following        年內，本集團從事以下主營業務：
     principal activities:


     •      Community space living consumption services              •   社區空間居住消費服務
     •      Commercial and urban space integrated services           •   商企和城市空間綜合服務
     •      AIoT and BPaaS solution services                         •   AIoT及BPaaS解決方案服務

     “China Vanke”), a public company established in the PRC         業」，一家於中國成立的上市公司，其

              2.4 Material accounting policies                                   2.4 重要會計政策

                     Investments in associates and joint ventures                   於聯營公司及合營企業之投資

                     An associate is an entity in which the Group has a             聯營公司是指本集團於其擁有
                     long-term interest of generally not less than 20%              長期權益（通常不低於股權表決
                     of the equity voting rights and over which it has              權 的 2 0 % ）， 並 對 其 有 重 大 影
                     significant influence. Significant influence is the            響的主體。重大影響力指有權
                     power to participate in the financial and operating            參與投資對象的財務及營運決
                     policy decisions of the investee, but is not control           策，但對該等決策並無控制權
                     or joint control over those policies.                          或共同控制權。

                     A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement                 合營企業是指共同控制一項安
                     whereby the parties that have joint control of the             排的參與方對該合營企業的淨
                     arrangement have rights to the net assets of the               資產享有權利的合營安排。共
                     joint venture. Joint control is the contractually              同控制是指按合同約定分享對
                     agreed sharing of control of an arrangement,                   一項安排的控制權，並且在相
                     which exists only when decisions about the relevant            關活動的決策中要求分享控制
                     activities require the unanimous consent of the                權的參與方一致同意時才存在。
                     parties sharing control.


                     The Group’s investments in associates and joint                本集團於聯營公司及合營企業
                     ventures are stated in the consolidated statement              之投資在合併財務狀況表中
                     of financial position at the Group’s share of net              以權益會計法按本集團應佔資
                     assets under the equity method of accounting, less             產淨值扣除任何減值虧損後列
                     any impairment losses. Adjustments are made to                 示。已作出調整以使任何可能
                     bring into line any dissimilar accounting policies             存在的不同會計政策與此一致。
                     that may exist.

          other components of non-controlling interests are              所有其他組成部分均按公允價

          Fair value measurement                                             公允價值計量

          The Group measures its equity investments                          本集團於各報告期末按公允價
          designated at fair value through other                             值計量其指定為以公允價值計
          comprehensive income, financial assets at fair                     量且其變動計入其他全面收益
          value through profit or loss, financial liability at fair          的股權投資及以公允價值計量
          value through profit or loss and derivative financial              且其變動計入當期損益的金融
          instruments at fair value at the end of each                       資產、以公允價值計量且其變
          reporting period. Fair value is the price that would               動計入當期損益的金融負債及
          be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a                 衍生金融工具。公允價值是指
          liability in an orderly transaction between market                 市場參與者在計量日的有序交
          participants at the measurement date. The fair                     易中，出售資產所收取或轉讓
          value measurement is based on the presumption                      負債所支付的價格。公允價值
          that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer                 計量乃基於假設出售資產或轉
          the liability takes place either in the principal                  讓負債的交易於資產或負債的
          market for the asset or liability, or in the absence               主要市場或（在無主要市場情況
          of a principal market, in the most advantageous                    下）於資產或負債的最有利市場
          market for the asset or liability. The principal or the            進行。主要或最有利市場應當
          most advantageous market must be accessible by                     是本集團能夠進入的市場。資
          the Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability               產或負債的公允價值乃基於市
          is measured using the assumptions that market                      場參與者為資產或負債定價時
          participants would use when pricing the asset or                   所用之假設計量（假設市場參與
          liability, assuming that market participants act in                者依照其最佳經濟利益行事）。
          their economic best interest.


          A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset                  非金融資產的公允價值計量須
          takes into account a market participant’s ability to               計及市場參與者通過使用該資
          generate economic benefits by using the asset in                   產的最高及最佳用途，或將該
          its highest and best use or by selling it to another               資產出售予將使用其最高及最
          market participant that would use the asset in its                 佳用途的另一市場參與者而產
          highest and best use.                                              生經濟利益的能力。

                               liabilities                                                整）

                               observable, either directly or indirectly                  數據的估值技術

                               the lowest level input that is significant                 言屬重大的不可觀察最
                               to the fair value measurement is                           低層級輸入數據的估值
                               unobservable                                               技術

                     Group determines whether transfers have occurred              各報告期末通過重新評估分類

          to which the asset belongs.                                      金產生單位的可收回金額。

          In testing a cash-generating unit for impairment, a              在對現金產生單位進行減值測
          portion of the carrying amount of a corporate asset              試時，一部分公司資產（例如總
          (e.g., a headquarters building) is allocated to an               部大樓）的賬面值分配予個別現
          individual cash-generating unit if it can be allocated           金產生單位，前提是其可在合

                                                                           位組別。

          An impairment loss is recognised only if the                     減值虧損僅於資產賬面值超過
          carrying amount of an asset exceeds its recoverable              其可收回金額時確認。在評估

          Impairment of financial assets (continued)                      金融資產減值（續）

          General approach                                                一般方法

          ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit                   預期信貸虧損於兩個階段確
          exposures for which there has not been a                        認。就自初始確認以來信貸風
          significant increase in credit risk since initial               險並無顯著增加的信貸風險項
          recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses                目而言，預期信貸虧損為就未
          that result from default events that are possible               來 12 個月內可能發生的違約事
          within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For                 件產生的信貸虧損計提撥備（12

                     relating to a provision is presented in the statement          的開支於扣除任何補償後於損

                     financial reporting purposes.

          to the net carrying amount of the financial asset.             率確認。

          shareholders’ right to receive payment has been                利確立，而與股息相關的經濟

          the Group and the amount of the dividend can be

              straight-line basis to write off the cost of each item of           物業、廠房及設備以及無形資產的成
              property, plant and equipment and intangible asset to its           本於其估計使用年期內撇減至其剩餘
              residual value over its estimated useful life. The estimated        價值。本集團將物業、廠房及設備項
              useful lives and dates that the Group places the items of           目投產使用後的估計使用年期及日期
              property, plant and equipment into productive use reflect           反映了董事對本集團計劃通過使用本
              the directors’ estimate of the periods that the Group               集團的物業、廠房及設備以及無形資
              intends to derive future economic benefits from the use of          產獲得未來經濟利益的估計期間。
              the Group’s property, plant and equipment and intangible
              assets.

              and unabsorbed depreciation allowances to the extent                他可扣減暫時差額的未來應課稅利
              that it is probable that future taxable profits would be            潤，則就所有未動用稅項虧損及未吸
              available against which the losses and other deductible             納折舊撥備確認遞延稅項資產。在釐
              temporary differences could be utilised. Significant                定可確認的遞延稅項資產數額時，須
              management judgement is required to determine the                   根據可能產生未來應課稅利潤的時間
              amount of deferred tax assets that could be recognised,             和水平連同未來稅項計劃戰略作出重
              based on the likely timing and extent of future taxable             大管理層判斷。
              profits together with future tax planning strategies.

     Impairment of non-financial assets                                     非金融資產（商譽除外）減值
     (other than goodwill)

     The Group assesses whether there are any indicators                    本集團於各報告期末評估所有非金融
     of impairment for all non-financial assets at the end of               資產是否存在任何減值跡象。當有跡
     each reporting period. Non-financial assets are tested for             象顯示賬面值可能無法收回時，對非
     impairment when there are indicators that the carrying                 金融資產進行減值測試。當資產或現
     amounts may not be recoverable. An impairment exists                   金產生單位的賬面值超過其可收回金
     when the carrying value of an asset or a cash-generating               額（即其公允價值減出售成本與其使
     unit exceeds its recoverable amount, which is the higher of            用 價 值 兩 者 中 的 較 高 者 ）， 則 存 在 減
     its fair value less costs of disposal and its value-in-use. The        值。公允價值減出售成本的計算乃基
     calculation of the fair value less costs of disposal is based          於類似資產於具有約束力的銷售交易
     on available data from binding sales transactions of similar           中的可用數據或可觀察的市場價格，
     assets or observable market prices less incremental costs              再減去出售資產的增量成本。當計算
     for disposing of the asset. When value-in-use calculations             使用價值時，管理層必須估計來自資
     are undertaken, management must estimate the expected                  產或現金產生單位的預期未來現金流
     future cash flows from the asset or cash-generating unit               量，並選擇合適的貼現率以計算該等
     and choose a suitable discount rate in order to calculate              現金流量的現值。
     the present value of those cash flows.


     Leases — Estimating the incremental                                    租賃 － 估計增量借款利率
     borrowing rate

     The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate                   本集團無法隨時釐定租賃中隱含的利
     implicit in a lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental             率，因此，其使用增量借款利率計量
     borrowing rate (“IBR”) to measure lease liabilities. The IBR           租賃負債。增量借款利率為本集團於
     is the rate of interest that the Group would have to pay to            類似經濟環境中為取得與使用權資
     borrow over a similar term, and with a similar security, the           產價值相近的資產，而以類似抵押品
     funds necessary to obtain an asset of a similar value to               於類似期間借入所需資金應支付的利
     the right-of-use asset in a similar economic environment.              率。因此，增量借款利率反映了本集
     The IBR therefore reflects what the Group “would have                  團「應支付」的利率，當無可觀察的利
     to pay”, which requires estimation when no observable                  率時（例如就未訂立融資交易的附屬
     rates are available (such as for subsidiaries that do not              公司而言）或當須對利率進行調整以
     enter into financing transactions) or when it needs to be              反映租賃的條款及條件時，則須作出
     adjusted to reflect the terms and conditions of the lease.             估計。

      11. INCOME TAX EXPENSE                                                11. 所得稅開支
          The Group is subject to income tax on an entity basis on profit      本集團須就產生自或源自本集團成員
          arising in or derived from the jurisdictions in which members        公司所處及經營所在的司法管轄區的
          of the Group are domiciled and operate.                              溢利按主體基準繳納所得稅。

          The provision for Mainland China income tax was based on             中國內地所得稅的撥備乃根據《中華
          a statutory rate of 25% (2023: 25%) of the taxable profits           人民共和國所得稅法》及相關規定基
          during the year as determined in accordance with the PRC             於年內應課稅利潤的25%（2023 年：
          Income Tax Law and the respective regulations.                       25%）法定稅率釐定。

          Under the Law of the PRC on Enterprise Income Tax (“EIT”)           根據《中華人民共和國企業所得稅法》
          and Implementation Regulation of the Enterprise Income Tax          及《 企 業 所 得 稅 法 實 施 條 例 》， 中 國
          Law, the tax rate of the PRC subsidiaries is (i) 25% (2023:         附 屬 公 司 ( i ) 稅 率 為 2 5 %（ 2 0 2 3 年 ：
          25%), or (ii) 15% (2023: 15%) if the subsidiaries are qualified     25%）；或(ii)如附屬公司被認定為高
          as high and new technology enterprises or registered in the         新技術企業，或在中國西部地區、深
          western region, Shenzhen Qianhai region and Guangdong               圳前海地區及廣東橫琴地區註冊成
          Hengqin region of the PRC and fulfil certain requirements.          立且符合相關規定，則稅率為15%
                                                                              （2023年：15%）。

          The subsidiary incorporated in Hong Kong is subject to Hong          於香港註冊成立的附屬公司於年內在
          Kong profits tax at the rate of 16.5% (2023: 16.5%) on any           香港產生的任何估計應課稅利潤須按
          estimated assessable profits arising in Hong Kong during the         16.5%（2023 年：16.5%）的稅率繳
          year. No provision for Hong Kong profits tax has been made           納香港利得稅。由於本集團於年內並
          as the Group has no assessable profits derived from or earned        無在香港產生或賺取應課稅利潤，故
          in Hong Kong during the year.                                        並無就香港利得稅作出撥備。

      11. INCOME TAX EXPENSE (continued)                                 11. 所得稅開支（續）
           The Group has elected to file combined tax return               本集團已選擇提交合併報稅表，併入
           incorporating assessable profit and tax losses attributable     本集團應佔以及本集團按管理酬金制
           to the Group as well as certain properties which are            管理的若干物業的應課稅利潤及稅項
           managed by the Group on management remuneration                 虧損。由於此安排，本集團可暫時動
           basis. As a result of such arrangement, the Group is able       用管理物業的稅項虧損，從而推遲支
           to temporarily utilise tax losses from managing properties,     付若干撥備。動用該等管理物業的稅
           resulting in deferral of payment of certain provision. The      項虧損及本集團推遲支付企業所得稅
           utilisation of such tax losses from managing properties and     撥備對報告期間的合併收益表並無影
           the deferral of the Group’s payment of EIT provision had        響。就財務會計而言，本集團已按其
           no effect on the consolidated income statement during           物業管理附屬公司的適用稅率，基於
           the reporting period. For financial accounting purposes,        應課稅利潤借記所得稅開支並貸記稅
           the Group has made relevant provision by debiting the           項負債，以便作出相關撥備。
           income tax expense and crediting tax liabilities based
           on assessable profits at the applicable tax rates of their
           property management subsidiaries.

23. TRADE AND RETENTION RECEIVABLES                              23. 貿易應收款項及應收保證
   (continued)                                                       金（續）
   The Group’s trading terms with its customers are mainly         本集團與其客戶的貿易條款主要以信
   on credit. The credit term is normally decided on a case-       貸為基礎。信貸期通常在產品驗收或
   by-case basis upon the acceptance of the products or            服務完成後逐案決定。信貸期通常為
   the completion of service. The credit period is generally       一個月，主要客戶最長可延至三個
   one month, extending up to three months for major               月。鑒於上文所述以及本集團貿易應
   customers. In view of the aforementioned and the fact           收款項及應收保證金與眾多不同客戶
   that the Group’s trade and retention receivables relate         有關的事實，故並無重大集中的信貸
   to a large number of diversified customers, there is no         風險。貿易應收款項不計息。
   significant concentration of credit risk. Trade receivables

31. RESERVES                                                      31. 儲備

   The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements             於當前及過往年度，本集團的儲備及
   therein for the current and prior years are presented in          其變動金額呈列於財務報表第 186 至
   the consolidated statement of changes in equity on pages          187頁的合併權益變動表。
   186 to 187 of the financial statements.


   (i)     Capital reserve                                           (i)     資本儲備

           The capital reserve of the Group represents 1) the                本集團的資本儲備是指：1)已
           difference between the par value of the shares                    發行股份面值與收到的對價
           issued and the consideration received; 2) the excess              之間的差額；2)對價超過已收
           of the consideration over the carrying amount of                  購 ╱ 出售的非控股權益的賬面
           the non-controlling interests acquired/disposed;                  值部分；及 3）已授出的以股份
           and 3) the fair value of the share-base payment                   為基礎的付款的公允價值。
           granted.


   (ii)    Fair value reserve of financial assets at fair value      (ii)    以公允價值計量且其變動計入
           through other comprehensive income                                其他全面收益的金融資產的公
                                                                             允價值儲備

           The fair value reserve of financial assets at fair                以公允價值計量且其變動計入
           value through other comprehensive income                          其他全面收益的金融資產的公
           comprises all revaluation changes arising from the                允價值儲備包括指定為以公允
           equity instruments designated at fair value through               價值計量且其變動計入其他全
           other comprehensive income.                                       面收益的權益工具產生的所有
                                                                             重估變動。

   (iii)   Exchange fluctuation reserve                              (iii)   匯率波動儲備

           The exchange fluctuation reserve represents                       匯率波動儲備是指功能貨幣與
           exchange differences arising from the translation                 本集團列報貨幣不同的海外業
           of the financial statements of foreign operations                 務的財務報表折算所產生的匯
           whose functional currencies are different from the                率差異。
           Group’s presentation currency.

          HIERARCHY OF FINANCIAL                                          價值層級（續）
          INSTRUMENTS (continued)

          The Group’s finance department headed by the finance            本集團的財務部門由財務經理領導，
          manager is responsible for determining the policies and         負責確定金融工具公允價值計量的政

   OBJECTIVES AND POLICIES

   The Group’s principal financial instruments mainly include            本集團的主要金融工具主要包括受限
   restricted bank deposits, cash and cash equivalents, trade            制銀行存款、現金及現金等價物、貿
   and other receivables, trade and other payables, which                易及其他應收款項、貿易及其他應付
   arise directly from its operations. The Group has other               款項，均直接來自其經營業務。本集
   financial assets and liabilities such as financial assets at fair     團擁有其他金融資產及負債，如以公
   value through profit or loss, time deposits with original             允價值計量且其變動計入當期損益的
   maturity of over three months, lease liabilities, amounts             金融資產、原到期日超過三個月的定
   due to related parties and amounts due from related                   期存款、租賃負債、應付關聯方款項
   parties. The main purpose of these financial instruments is           及應收關聯方款項。該等金融工具的
   to raise finance for the Group’s operations.                          主要目的是為本集團的營運籌集資金。

   The main risks arising from the Group’s financial                     本集團金融工具產生的主要風險為外
   instruments are foreign currency risk, credit risk and                匯風險、信貸風險及流動性風險。董
   liquidity risk. The board of directors reviews and agrees             事會檢討及議定政策，以管理各項風
   policies for managing each of these risks and they are                險，概述如下。
   summarised below.


   Foreign currency risk                                                 外匯風險

   Foreign currency risk is the risk of loss resulting from              外匯風險是指因外幣匯率變動而造成
   changes in foreign exchange rates. Fluctuations in                    損失的風險。人民幣與本集團經營業
   exchange rates between RMB and other currencies                       務所用其他貨幣之間的匯率波動可能
   in which the Group conducts business may affect the                   會影響本集團的財務狀況及經營業
   Group’s financial condition and results of operations. As at          績。於報告期末，由於本集團並未持
   the end of the reporting period, since the Group did not              有任何以各經營單位功能貨幣以外的
   hold any financial instruments denominated in currencies              貨幣計值的金融工具，故本集團並未
   other than the functional currencies of the respective                面臨任何重大外匯風險。
   operating units, the Group did not have any significant
   exposure to the foreign currency risk.

          The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management        本集團資本管理的主要目標為保障本
          are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going     集團的持續經營能力及維持穩健的資
          concern and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to      本比率，以支持其業務並將股東價值
          support its business and maximise shareholders’ value.          最大化。

          The Group manages its capital structure and makes               本集團根據經濟狀況變化及相關資產
          adjustments to it in light of changes in economic               的風險特徵來管理及調整其資本架
          conditions and the risk characteristics of the underlying       構。為維持或調整資本架構，本集團
          assets. To maintain or adjust the capital structure, the        可能會調整向股東派發的股息、向股
          Group may adjust the dividend payment to shareholders,          東返還資本或發行新股。
          return capital to shareholders or issue new shares.

    borrowings minus cash and cash equivalents divided by          現金及現金等價物除以截至報告期末
